=> a_k = (B_k - ∑_{i=1}^{k-1} a_i a_{k-i})/(2a0).

Top-level helpers:

import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize, linprog

class? no.

def _fft_len(n):
    m=2*n-1
    return 1 << (m-1).bit_length()

def _normalize(a):
    s=a.sum()
    if s<=0: return np.ones(len(a))/len(a)
    return a/s

def _convolution(a, M=None):
    n=len(a); c_len=2*n-1
    if M is None: M=_fft_len(n)
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,M)
    c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,M)[:c_len]
    # remove tiny negatives
    # no full clip? c[c<0]=0
    return c

def _exact_score(a, M=None, c=None):
    n=len(a); S=a.sum()
    if S<=1e-15: return np.inf
    if c is None: c=_convolution(a,M)
    maxc=float(c.max())
    if maxc<=0: return np.inf
    return float(2.0*n*maxc/(S*S))

def _softmax(x):
    x=np.asarray(x)
    m=np.max(x)
    if not np.isfinite(m): m=0.0
    ex=np.exp(x - m)
    s=ex.sum()
    if s<=0 or not np.isfinite(s): return np.ones_like(x)/len(x)
    return ex/s

def _lse_obj(x, beta, M, n, obj_scale=1.0):
    # returns (val, grad)
    x_clip = np.clip(x, -50.0, 50.0)
    a = _softmax(x_clip)
    # objective value: 2n LSE
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, M)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A*A, M)[:2*n-1]
    cmax = float(c.max())
    if beta <= 0:
       L=cmax
    else:
       scaled = beta*(c-cmax)
       maxs = np.max(scaled) # should 0
       # Clip for safety
       scaled = np.clip(scaled, -700, 700)
       es = np.exp(scaled - maxs)
       L = cmax + maxs/beta + float(np.log(es.sum()))/beta? Wait logsumexp formula: if scaled already beta*(c-cmax), we can do raw scaled (0 at max), exp clipped. logsum = np.log(np.sum(np.exp(scaled - maxscaled))). Since maxscaled maybe 0. L = cmax + (maxscaled + logsum)/beta. If maxscaled=0, L=cmax + logsum/beta. If we subtract maxscaled, logsum = logsum of unshifted? Actually logsumexp(s)=m+log(sum(exp(s-m))). s = beta(c-cmax), m=0. We used scaled clip maybe m? Let m = maxscaled after clip maybe. L=cmax + (m+log(sum(exp(scaled-m))))/beta. Good.
    score = obj_scale*2*n*L
    # p
    if beta <=0: p = (c==cmax) average? Use p uniform over max.
    else:
       s = beta*(c-cmax)
       m = float(np.max(s))
       s = np.clip(s, -700+ m? Actually if m large, s-m <=0. We'll do s2=s-m; clip s2 [-700,700]? s2<=0. exp okay.
       es=np.exp(s2); p=es/es.sum()
    p_rev = p[::-1]
    PR = np.fft.rfft(p_rev, M)
    C = np.fft.irfft(A*PR, M)[:2*n-1]
    h = C[::-1][:n]
    ga = 4.0 * n * h
    mean = float(np.dot(a, ga))
    grad = a * (ga - mean)
    # clean nonfinite
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)): grad=np.zeros_like(a)
    val=score if finite else 1e18
    return val, grad

run_lse_lbfgs:
def run_lse_lbfgs(a0, alpha, M, n, maxfun=2000, tol_g=1e-5):
  c0=_convolution(a0,M); cmax0=float(c0.max()); if cmax0<=0: return a0
  beta = alpha / cmax0
  # maybe cap beta to 1e8 to avoid overflow? s=beta*c; max at cmax ~alpha. If alpha=10000, beta*cmax=10000, okay s-m <=0. no overflow. beta may large but fine.
  x0 = np.log(np.maximum(a0, np.finfo(float).tiny))
  x0 = np.clip(x0, -30,30)
  bounds=[(-30.0,30.0)]*n
  options={'maxiter': max(maxfun, 100), 'maxfun': maxfun, 'ftol':1e-12, 'gtol':1e-6, 'iprint':0}
  try:
    res=minimize(_lse_obj, x0, args=(beta,M,n), method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, bounds=bounds, options=options)
    a_new=_softmax(np.clip(res.x,-30,30)); a_new=normalize(a_new)
  except Exception:
    a_new=a0
  return a_new

We should perhaps not let minimize run indefinitely if maxfun large. Good.

But note: scipy minimize with bounds creates a lot of overhead; for each call it may evaluate at bounds? okay.

def _lp_direction(a, c, peaks, n, K, z_lower=-10.0):
   K=min(K,len(peaks))
   if K==0: return None
   rows=[] maybe prealloc.
   N=n+1
   A=np.empty((K,N), dtype=np.float64)
   b=np.empty(K)
   count=0
   for idx in peaks:
      k=int(idx)
      # if count reached break
      left=max(0,k-(n-1)); right=min(k,n-1)
      if left>right: continue
      g=np.zeros(n)
      i=np.arange(left,right+1)
      j=k-i
      # ensure j within 0..n-1

      # Since j array is descending but within. It works.

      row=A[count]
      row[:n]=g; row[-1]=-1.0
      b[count]=bcount
      count+=1
   if count==0: return None
   A=A[:count]; b=b[:count]
   c_obj=np.zeros(n+1); c_obj[-1]=1.0
   A_eq=np.ones(n+1); A_eq[-1]=0.0
   b_eq=np.ones(1)
   bounds=[(0.0,None)]*n + [(z_lower,None)]
   try:
      res=linprog(c_obj, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, A_eq=A_eq, b_eq=b_eq, bounds=bounds, method='highs', options={'presolve':True, 'dual_feasibility_tolerance':1e-9, 'primal_feasibility_tolerance':1e-9})
      if res.success or res.status in (0,2):? linprog success True if optimal. If not success but fun finite? Use if hasattr(res,'x') and res.x is not None and np.isfinite(res.x[-1]) maybe.
      a_lp=res.x[:n].copy()
      a_lp=np.maximum(a_lp,0)
      s=a_lp.sum()
      if s<=0: return None
      a_lp/=s
      return a_lp
   except Exception:
      return None

def _lp_polish(a0, M, n, max_iters, time_limit):

Interpolation top-level:
def _interp_seq(parent, target_n):
    old_n=parent.size
    if old_n==target_n: return parent.copy()
    if old_n==1: return full target
    x_old=np.linspace(0,1,old_n); x_new=np.linspace(0,1,target_n); a=np.interp(x_new,x_old,parent); return np.maximum(a,0)

def _optimize_sequence(a0, budget):
    a = np.asarray(a0,float).copy()
    a = np.maximum(a,0.0)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<1e-12: a=np.ones_like(a)/len(a)
    else: a=a/s
    n=len(a); M=_fft_len(n)
    best=a.copy(); best_sc=_exact_score(a,M)
    t_start=time.monotonic(); t_end=t_start+max(60.0,float(budget))
    print(f"Optimize seq n={n} budget={budget:.0f}s score0={best_sc:.8f}")
    # function budget based on n
    if n <= 2048: funnel=2500
    elif n <= 4096: funnel=1800
    elif n <= 8192: funnel=1000
    else: funnel=500
    # low alpha adapt
    for alpha in [5, 20, 80]:
        if time.monotonic() > t_end - 30: break
        a_new = _run_lse_lbfgs(a, alpha, M, n, maxfun=min(funnel, 1200))
        sc_new=_exact_score(a_new,M)
        if sc_new < best_sc:
            best=a_new.copy(); best_sc=sc_new
            a=a_new.copy()
        elif sc_new < best_sc + 1e-4:
            # keep current best? Maybe update a to new for continuation if finite and not worse too much
            if np.isfinite(sc_new): a=a_new.copy()
        print(...)
    # LP middle
    if time.monotonic() < t_end - 90:
       tl = min(180.0, max(30.0, 0.25*(t_end-time.monotonic())))
       a_lp, sc_lp = _lp_polish(a, M, n, max_iters=30, time_limit=tl, rng=np.random.default_rng(1))
       if sc_lp < best_sc: best=a_lp.copy(); best_sc=sc_lp
       if sc_lp < _exact_score(a,M) + 1e-6: a=a_lp.copy()
    # high alpha
    alphas=[200,600,1500,5000]
    # allocate remaining funnel * weights
    for alpha in alphas:
       now=time.monotonic(); rem=t_end-now-40
       if rem<30: break
       # increase funnel alpha factor
       mf=funnel*2 if alpha<=1500 else funnel*1.2
       # limit by estimated time? mf = min(mf, int(rem*2)?)
       a_new=_run_lse_lbfgs(a, alpha, M,n, maxfun=int(mf))
       sc_new=...
       update
       if no improvement twice? could continue
    # LP again
    if time.monotonic() < t_end - 60:
       tl=min(180.0, max(30.0,0.3*(t_end-time.monotonic())))
       a_lp,sc_lp=_lp_polish(best? or a? If best significantly better, use best as start; a maybe worse. Use max(best, a) start: start = best if best_sc <= score(a) else a.
       update
    # very high alpha if time left
    if time.monotonic() < t_end - 60:
       a_new=_run_lse_lbfgs(best, 20000, M,n,maxfun=funnel)
       update
    return best,best_sc

Now _lp_polish: uses rng. To avoid creating rng repeatedly inside (which uses OS entropy?) np.random.default_rng seed fast. We'll pass seed.

def _lp_polish(a0, M, n, max_iters, time_limit, seed=0):
  a=_normalize(a0)
  best=a.copy(); best_sc=_exact_score(a,M)
  rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
  t0=time.monotonic()
  no_improve=0
  K = 80 if n<=10000 else 50
  for it in range(max_iters):
    if time.monotonic()-t0 > time_limit: break
    c=_convolution(a,M)
    cmax=float(c.max())
    sc=2.0*n*cmax*(? a sum1)
    if sc < best_sc: best=a.copy(); best_sc=sc
    # top peaks
    if len(c) <= K: peaks=np.arange(len(c))
    else: peaks=np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
    # random variation if no improve > 4
    if no_improve > 4 and (2*no_improve < len(c)):
       # choose half top, half above 0.7 cmax random
       n_rand=min(K//2, max(0,int(len(c)*0.1)))
       high=np.where(c >= 0.7*cmax)[0]
       if len(high)>n_rand:
          rand_peaks=rng.choice(high, size=n_rand, replace=False)
          remaining=K-len(rand_peaks)
          top_peaks=peaks[-remaining:]? ensure unique

```
def _make_basic_starts(n):
    n=int(n)
    x=np.arange(n,dtype=float)
    starts=[]
    starts.append(np.ones(n))
    a=np.zeros(n); h=n//2; a[:h]=4.0; a[h:]=2.0; starts.append(a)
    a=np.zeros(n); p=max(1,int(0.4*n)); a[:p]=5.0; a[p:]=1.0; starts.append(a)
    starts.append(np.exp(0.9*x/n))
    starts.append(np.exp(-0.9*x/n))
    for m,phase in [(2,0.0),(3,0.7),(4,1.3)]:
        a=1.0+0.35*np.sin(2*np.pi*m*x/n+phase)
        a=np.maximum(a,1e-3)
        starts.append(a)
    a=1.0+0.8*np.tanh((x/n-0.5)*4.0)
    a=np.maximum(a,1e-3); starts.append(a)
    a=np.zeros(n); p1=n//5; p2=2*n//5; a[:p1]=3.0; a[p1:p2]=1.0; a[p2:]=2.0; starts.append(a)
    out=[]
    for a in starts:
       a=np.asarray(a,float)
       sc=np.inf
       try: s=a.sum(); if s>0: a=a/s; if np.all(np.isfinite(a)): out.append(a)
       except: pass
    return out
```

def _select_starts(a_list, M, top_k):
    arr=[]

       sc=_exact_score(a,M)
       arr.append((sc,a))

_line_search: if b is same as a? evaluate. We can reduce t list. Use old_sc. It returns best_a. If t=1 best, best_b = b.
```
def _line_search(a0,b,M,n,old_sc=None):
    old_sc = _exact_score(a0,M) if old_sc is None else old_sc
    best_a=a0.copy(); best_sc=old_sc
    for t in (1.0,0.8,0.6,0.4,0.2,0.1,0.05,0.02):
       at = (1.0-t)*a0 + t*b
       s=at.sum()
       if s<=0: continue
       at=at/s
       sc=_exact_score(at,M)
       if sc < best_sc:
           best_sc=sc; best_a=at.copy()
    return best_a,best_sc
```
Note when t=1, at=b normalized; if b sum1.

Now, `_run_lse_lbfgs`: If beta maybe inf due alpha/cmax? cmax small; set if not finite `beta=1e7`. Use `alpha` not used if cmax0<=0.
```
def _run_lse_lbfgs(a0, alpha, M, n, maxfun):
   a0=_normalize(a0)
   c0=_convolution(a0,M)
   cmax0=float(np.max(c0))
   if not np.isfinite(cmax0) or cmax0 <= 0: return a0,0
   beta = alpha / cmax0
   if not np.isfinite(beta) or beta <=0: beta=1e6
   x0 = np.log(np.maximum(a0, 1e-300)); x0=np.clip(x0,-40,40)
   bounds=[(-40.0,40.0)]*n
   options={'maxiter': max(100, 2*maxfun), 'maxfun': maxfun, 'ftol':1e-13, 'gtol':1e-6, 'iprint':0, 'maxls':50}
   try:
      res = minimize(_lse_obj, x0, args=(beta,M,n), method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, bounds=bounds, options=options)
   except Exception:
      return a0,0
   if res.x is None: return a0,0
   a_new = _softmax(np.clip(res.x,-40,40))
   a_new = _normalize(a_new)
   nfev = int(getattr(res,'nfev', maxfun))
   return a_new, nfev
```
Maybe if maxfun is very large (10000), maxiter=20000. Good.

```python
"""
Search for low-peak nonnegative convolution-square sequences.

Primary ideas:
- Normalize sequences to sum 1 and work on the simplex.
- Smooth the max-convolution objective with a relative-temperature log-sum-exp.
- Optimize in log/softmax coordinates with L-BFGS-B, using exact FFT convolutions.
- Use an affine-approximation / linear-programming active-set heuristic to move
  mass away from the currently most urgent convolution coefficients.
- Multi-length exploration from the parent construction, with a final polish.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize, linprog


def _fft_len(n):
    n = int(n)
    m = 2 * n - 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def _normalize(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.ones(len(a), dtype=np.float64) / max(1, len(a))
    return a / s


def _convolution(a, M=None):
    n = int(len(a))
    c_len = 2 * n - 1
    if M is None:
        M = _fft_len(n)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, M)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, M)[:c_len]
    return c


def _exact_score(a, M=None):
    n = int(len(a))
    S = float(a.sum())
    if S <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(S):
        return np.inf
    c = _convolution(a, M)
    Mx = float(np.max(c))
    if Mx <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(Mx):
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * n * Mx / (S * S))


def _softmax(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    mx = float(np.max(x))
    if not np.isfinite(mx):
        mx = 0.0
    ex = np.exp(x - mx)
    s = float(ex.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.ones_like(x, dtype=np.float64) / float(len(x))
    return ex / s


def _lse_obj(x, beta, M, n):
    """
    Objective and gradient for 2*n * logsumexp(beta*c)/beta.
    Coordinates are softmax x, so the returned gradient is w.r.t. x.
    """
    if len(x) != n:
        return 1e30, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    x = np.clip(np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64), -40.0, 40.0)
    a = _softmax(x)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
        return 1e30, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    c_len = 2 * n - 1
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, M)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, M)[:c_len]
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(c)):
        return 1e30, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    if cmax <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(cmax):
        return 1e30, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    if beta <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(beta):
        val = 2.0 * n * cmax
        active = np.where(np.isclose(c, cmax, rtol=1e-12, atol=0.0))[0]
        if len(active) == 0:
            p = np.full(c_len, 1.0 / c_len, dtype=np.float64)
        else:
            p = np.zeros(c_len, dtype=np.float64)
            p[active] = 1.0 / len(active)
    else:
        s = beta * (c - cmax)
        # Since cmax is the maximum, s should be <= 0 up to tiny roundoff.
        s = np.clip(s, -700.0, 0.0)
        p = np.exp(s)
        ps = float(p.sum())
        if ps <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(ps):
            p = np.full(c_len, 1.0 / c_len, dtype=np.float64)
        else:
            p = p / ps
        logsum = float(np.log(ps))
        L = cmax + logsum / beta
        val = 2.0 * n * L

    # h_i = sum_k p_k a_{k-i} for valid k-i in [0,n-1].
    p_rev = p[::-1]
    PR = np.fft.rfft(p_rev, M)
    C = np.fft.irfft(A * PR, M)[:c_len]
    h = C[::-1][:n]
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64)

    ga = 4.0 * n * h
    mean = float(np.dot(a, ga))
    grad = a * (ga - mean)

    if not np.isfinite(val):
        val = 1e30
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
        grad = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    return float(val), grad


def _run_lse_lbfgs(a0, alpha, M, n, maxfun):
    """
    Run L-BFGS on softmax coordinates with a LSE objective.

    alpha is the relative temperature: beta = alpha / max(c) at the start.
    Returns (normalized_a, number_of_function_evaluations).
    """
    a0 = _normalize(a0)
    c0 = _convolution(a0, M)
    cmax0 = float(np.max(c0))
    if cmax0 <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(cmax0):
        return a0, 0

    beta = float(alpha) / cmax0
    if not np.isfinite(beta) or beta <= 1.0:
        beta = 1e6

    x0 = np.log(np.maximum(a0, 1e-300))
    x0 = np.clip(x0, -40.0, 40.0)
    x0 = np.asarray(x0, dtype=np.float64)

    bounds = [(-40.0, 40.0)] * int(n)
    options = {
        "maxiter": max(100, 2 * int(maxfun)),
        "maxfun": int(maxfun),
        "ftol": 1e-13,
        "gtol": 1e-6,
        "maxls": 50,
        "iprint": 0,
    }

    try:
        res = minimize(
            _lse_obj,
            x0,
            args=(beta, M, n),
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            jac=True,
            bounds=bounds,
            options=options,
        )
    except Exception:
        return a0, 0

    if res is None or not hasattr(res, "x") or res.x is None:
        return a0, 0

    a_new = _softmax(np.clip(res.x, -40.0, 40.0))
    a_new = _normalize(a_new)

    nfev = int(getattr(res, "nfev", int(maxfun)))
    return a_new, max(0, nfev)


def _lp_direction(a, c, peaks, n, K):
    """
    Solve min max_j (linearized c_j) over the simplex using HiGHS/LP.
    Returns a feasible direction point a_lp (sum 1, nonnegative), or None.
    """
    peaks = np.asarray(peaks, dtype=int)
    if peaks.size == 0:
        return None
    K = int(min(K, peaks.size))
    if K <= 0:
        return None
    peaks = np.unique(peaks[:K])
    K = peaks.size
    if K == 0:
        return None

    N = n + 1
    A_ub = np.zeros((K, N), dtype=np.float64)
    b_ub = np.zeros(K, dtype=np.float64)

    count = 0
    for kk in peaks:
        k = int(kk)
        if k < 0 or k >= 2 * n - 1:
            continue
        left = max(0, k - (n - 1))
        right = min(k, n - 1)
        if left > right:
            continue

        row = A_ub[count]
        row *= 0.0
        i_idx = np.arange(left, right + 1, dtype=int)
        j_idx = k - i_idx

        # Scaled gradient of c_k: multiply by n for solver conditioning.
        row[left:right + 1] = 2.0 * float(n) * a[j_idx]
        row[-1] = -1.0

        ck = float(c[k])
        if not np.isfinite(ck) or ck < 0.0:
            ck = 0.0
        b_ub[count] = float(n) * ck
        count += 1

    if count == 0:
        return None

    A_ub = A_ub[:count]
    b_ub = b_ub[:count]

    c_obj = np.zeros(N, dtype=np.float64)
    c_obj[-1] = 1.0

    A_eq = np.ones(N, dtype=np.float64)
    A_eq[-1] = 0.0
    b_eq = np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)

    bounds = [(0.0, None)] * n + [(-1000.0, None)]

    try:
        res = linprog(
            c_obj,
            A_ub=A_ub,
            b_ub=b_ub,
            A_eq=A_eq,
            b_eq=b_eq,
            bounds=bounds,
            method="highs",
            options={
                "presolve": True,
                "time_limit": 2.0,
            },
        )
    except Exception:
        return None

    if res is None or not hasattr(res, "x") or res.x is None:
        return None
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
        return None

    a_lp = np.asarray(res.x[:n], dtype=np.float64)
    a_lp = np.maximum(a_lp, 0.0)
    s = float(a_lp.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return None
    a_lp = a_lp / s
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(a_lp)):
        return None
    return a_lp


def _line_search(a0, b, M, n, old_sc=None):
    old_sc = _exact_score(a0, M) if old_sc is None else float(old_sc)
    best_a = a0.copy()
    best_sc = old_sc

    # Sample a handful of points on the segment. Linearized LP directions can
    # be curved in exact objective; this is cheap and robust.
    for t in (1.0, 0.85, 0.7, 0.5, 0.35, 0.2, 0.1, 0.05, 0.02):
        at = (1.0 - t) * a0 + t * b
        s = float(at.sum())
        if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
            continue
        at = at / s
        sc = _exact_score(at, M)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = at.copy()

    return best_a, float(best_sc)


def _lp_polish(a0, M, n, max_iters, time_limit, seed=1):
    a = _normalize(a0)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = _exact_score(a, M)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    no_improve = 0

    K = 80 if n <= 10000 else 50
    K = int(min(K, max(20, 2 * n - 1)))

    for it in range(int(max_iters)):
        if time.monotonic() - t0 > float(time_limit):
            break

        c = _convolution(a, M)
        cmax = float(np.max(c))
        if cmax <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(cmax):
            break
        sc = 2.0 * n * cmax  # a is normalized

        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = float(sc)
            best_a = a.copy()

        if c.size <= K:
            peaks = np.arange(c.size)
        else:
            peaks = np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]

        # Occasionally mix in other near-peak coefficients to reduce cycling.
        if no_improve > 4 and c.max() > 0:
            n_rand = min(K // 2, max(0, int(0.15 * len(c))))
            if n_rand > 0:
                high = np.where(c >= 0.7 * cmax)[0]
                if high.size > n_rand:
                    rand_peaks = rng.choice(high, size=n_rand, replace=False)
                    rem = K - rand_peaks.size
                    if rem > 0:
                        top_peaks = peaks[-rem:]
                    else:
                        top_peaks = np.empty(0, dtype=int)
                    peaks = np.unique(np.concatenate((rand_peaks, top_peaks)))[:K]

        a_lp = _lp_direction(a, c, peaks, n, K)
        if a_lp is None:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve > 12:
                break
            continue

        a_new, sc_new = _line_search(a, a_lp, M, n, old_sc=sc)

        if np.isfinite(sc_new) and sc_new < best_sc - 1e-12:
            best_sc = float(sc_new)
            best_a = a_new.copy()
            a = a_new.copy()
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve > max(15, int(max_iters) // 3):
                break

    return best_a, float(best_sc)


def _interpolate_sequence(parent, target_n):
    parent = np.asarray(parent, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    target_n = int(target_n)
    if parent.size <= 0 or target_n <= 0:
        return np.ones(target_n, dtype=np.float64)
    if parent.size == target_n:
        return parent.copy()
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, parent.size)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, target_n)
    a = np.interp(x_new, x_old, np.maximum(parent, 0.0))
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    return _normalize(a)


def _make_basic_starts(n):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return []
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    starts = []

    starts.append(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))

    # Two-block skewed starts.
    a = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    h = n // 2
    a[:h] = 4.0
    a[h:] = 2.0
    starts.append(a)

    a = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    p = max(1, int(0.40 * n))
    a[:p] = 5.0
    a[p:] = 1.0
    starts.append(a)

    # Monotone / exponential starts.
    starts.append(np.exp(0.8 * (x / n)))
    starts.append(np.exp(-0.8 * (x / n)))

    # Bounded sinusoidal perturbations of uniform.
    for m, phase in ((2, 0.0), (3, 0.7), (4, 1.3)):
        a = 1.0 + 0.35 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * m * (x / n) + phase)
        starts.append(np.maximum(a, 1e-3))

    # Smooth asymmetric step.
    a = 1.0 + 0.8 * np.tanh((x / n - 0.5) * 4.0)
    starts.append(np.maximum(a, 1e-3))

    # Three-block start.
    a = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    p1 = max(1, n // 5)
    p2 = max(p1 + 1, (2 * n) // 5)
    a[:p1] = 3.0
    a[p1:p2] = 1.0
    a[p2:] = 2.0
    starts.append(a)

    out = []
    for a in starts:
        a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
        a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
        a = _normalize(a)
        if a.size == n and np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
            out.append(a)
    return out


def _best_start(starts):
    best_a = None
    best_sc = np.inf
    for a in starts:
        try:
            sc = _exact_score(a)
        except Exception:
            sc = np.inf
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = a
    return best_a


def _optimize_sequence(a0, budget):
    a = _normalize(a0)
    n = int(len(a))
    if n <= 0:
        a = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64)
        n = len(a)
    M = _fft_len(n)

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = _exact_score(a, M)

    t_start = time.monotonic()
    t_end = t_start + max(60.0, float(budget))
    print(f"  optimize start: n={n}, score={best_sc:.10f}, budget={float(budget):.0f}s")

    # Calibrate function evaluations per second for adaptive maxfun.
    ccal = _convolution(a, M)
    cmaxcal = float(np.max(ccal))
    if cmaxcal <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(cmaxcal):
        cmaxcal = 1e-6
    xcal = np.clip(np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300)), -40.0, 40.0)
    beta_cal = 100.0 / cmaxcal
    tc = time.monotonic()
    for _ in range(3):
        _lse_obj(xcal, beta_cal, M, n)
    tc = max(1e-4, time.monotonic() - tc)
    cfs = 3.0 / tc
    cfs = float(np.clip(cfs, 1.0, 10000.0))

    if n <= 2048:
        fun_cap = 5000
    elif n <= 4096:
        fun_cap = 3000
    elif n <= 8192:
        fun_cap = 1800
    else:
        fun_cap = 900

    def _try_lse(alpha, target_seconds):
        nonlocal_rank = None  # placeholder not used; Python allows nested variables but no nested helpers.
        pass

    # The above placeholder is intentionally empty and unused; actual logic below uses only top-level helpers.

    def _none_helper():
        return None

    # Low-alpha smoothing and LP.
    for alpha in (10.0, 50.0, 200.0):
        now = time.monotonic()
        rem = t_end - now - 40.0
        if rem < 35.0:
            break
        target = min(60.0, 0.55 * rem)
        mf = int(max(600, min(fun_cap, max(600, int(cfs * target)))))
        t0 = time.monotonic()
        a_new, nfev = _run_lse_lbfgs(a, alpha, M, n, mf)
        dt = max(1e-4, time.monotonic() - t0)
        if nfev > 0:
            cfs = 0.6 * cfs + 0.4 * (float(nfev) / dt)
        sc_new = _exact_score(a_new, M)
        if np.isfinite(sc_new) and sc_new < best_sc:
            best_sc = float(sc_new)
            best_a = a_new.copy()
            a = a_new.copy()
            print(f"    LSE alpha={alpha:g}: {best_sc:.10f}")
        else:
            if np.isfinite(sc_new) and sc_new <= best_sc + 1e-5:
                a = a_new.copy()

    if time.monotonic() < t_end - 100.0:
        tl = min(180.0, max(30.0, 0.25 * (t_end - time.monotonic())))
        if _exact_score(a, M) > best_sc + 1e-8:
            a = best_a.copy()
        a_lp, sc_lp = _lp_polish(a, M, n, max_iters=30, time_limit=tl, seed=101)
        if np.isfinite(sc_lp) and sc_lp < best_sc:
            best_sc = float(sc_lp)
            best_a = a_lp.copy()
            a = a_lp.copy()
            print(f"    LP middle: {best_sc:.10f}")

    # Mid/high alpha refinement.
    for alpha in (800.0, 3000.0, 10000.0):
        now = time.monotonic()
        rem = t_end - now - 45.0
        if rem < 35.0:
            break
        target = min(100.0, 0.55 * rem)
        mf = int(max(700, min(fun_cap, max(700, int(cfs * target)))))
        t0 = time.monotonic()
        a_new, nfev = _run_lse_lbfgs(a, alpha, M, n, mf)
        dt = max(1e-4, time.monotonic() - t0)
        if nfev > 0:
            cfs = 0.6 * cfs + 0.4 * (float(nfev) / dt)
        sc_new = _exact_score(a_new, M)
        if np.isfinite(sc_new) and sc_new < best_sc:
            best_sc = float(sc_new)
            best_a = a_new.copy()
            a = a_new.copy()
            print(f"    LSE alpha={alpha:g}: {best_sc:.10f}")
        else:
            if np.isfinite(sc_new) and sc_new <= best_sc + 1e-5:
                a = a_new.copy()
            if alpha >= 10000.0:
                break

    # Final active-set polish.
    if time.monotonic() < t_end - 70.0:
        tl = min(220.0, max(40.0, 0.30 * (t_end - time.monotonic())))
        if _exact_score(a, M) > best_sc + 1e-8:
            a = best_a.copy()
        a_lp, sc_lp = _lp_polish(a, M, n, max_iters=45, time_limit=tl, seed=202)
        if np.isfinite(sc_lp) and sc_lp < best_sc:
            best_sc = float(sc_lp)
            best_a = a_lp.copy()
            a = a_lp.copy()
            print(f"    LP final: {best_sc:.10f}")

    if time.monotonic() < t_end - 50.0 and best_sc < np.inf:
        rem = t_end - time.monotonic() - 30.0
        if rem > 30.0:
            mf = int(max(500, min(fun_cap, int(cfs * 45.0))))
            a_new, _ = _run_lse_lbfgs(best_a, 25000.0, M, n, mf)
            sc_new = _exact_score(a_new, M)
            if np.isfinite(sc_new) and sc_new < best_sc:
                best_sc = float(sc_new)
                best_a = a_new.copy()
                print(f"    very-high-alpha: {best_sc:.10f}")

    if not np.isfinite(best_sc):
        best_a = a.copy()
        best_sc = _exact_score(best_a, M)

    print(f"  optimize end:   n={n}, score={best_sc:.10f}")
    return best_a, float(best_sc)


def propose_candidate():
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    total_budget = 2400.0
    deadline = t_start + total_budget - 50.0

    parent = None
    try:
        if "height_sequence_1" in globals():
            p = np.asarray(globals()["height_sequence_1"], dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
            if p.size >= 32 and p.sum() > 1e-300 and np.all(np.isfinite(p)):
                parent = p
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    best_a = None
    best_sc = np.inf

    specs = []

    if parent is not None:
        N = int(parent.size)
        pn = _normalize(parent)

        # Evaluate original parent if not too expensive; this preserves any
        # existing best construction even if we later downsample it to search.
        if N <= 50000:
            try:
                scp = _exact_score(pn, _fft_len(N))
                if np.isfinite(scp) and scp < best_sc:
                    best_sc = float(scp)
                    best_a = pn.copy()
                print(f"Parent original: n={N}, score={best_sc if best_sc < np.inf else float('nan'):.10f}")
            except Exception:
                pass

        if N <= 16384:
            p_work = pn.copy()
        else:
            p_work = _interpolate_sequence(parent, 8192)

        specs.append(("parent", p_work, 0.60))

        if abs(len(p_work) - 8192) > 128:
            specs.append(("interp8192", _interpolate_sequence(parent, 8192), 0.25))

        if N > 6000 and abs(len(p_work) - 4096) > 128:
            specs.append(("interp4096", _interpolate_sequence(parent, 4096), 0.15))

        # A different basin at a near size.
        bn = 8192 if len(p_work) < 10000 else 4096
        starts = _make_basic_starts(bn)
        sb = _best_start(starts)
        if sb is not None:
            specs.append(("basic", sb, 0.20))

    else:
        for n, frac in ((4096, 0.45), (8192, 0.55)):
            starts = _make_basic_starts(n)
            b = _best_start(starts)
            if b is not None:
                specs.append((f"basic{n}", b, frac))

    if not specs:
        a = np.ones(4096, dtype=np.float64)
        best_a = a
        best_sc = _exact_score(a)

    total_frac = 0.0
    for _, _, fr in specs:
        total_frac += float(fr)
    if total_frac <= 0.0:
        total_frac = 1.0

    # Limit effective work time to leave room for final verification.
    for label, a_cand, frac in specs:
        now = time.monotonic()
        remaining = deadline - now
        if remaining < 180.0:
            break
        esc = _exact_score(a_cand)
        if np.isfinite(esc) and esc < best_sc:
            best_sc = float(esc)
            best_a = _normalize(a_cand)
            print(f"New best before optimize ({label}): {best_sc:.10f}, n={len(best_a)}")

        if not np.isfinite(esc):
            continue

        # Skip obviously hopeless alternate candidates if we already have a good one.
        if best_sc < 2.0 and esc > best_sc + 0.05:
            continue

        budget = (remaining - 90.0) * (float(frac) / total_frac)
        budget = float(np.clip(budget, 120.0, max(120.0, remaining * 0.85)))
        try:
            a_opt, sc_opt = _optimize_sequence(a_cand, budget)
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"candidate {label} failed: {e}")
            continue

        if np.isfinite(sc_opt) and sc_opt < best_sc:
            best_sc = float(sc_opt)
            best_a = _normalize(a_opt)
            print(f"Updated global best after {label}: {best_sc:.10f}, n={len(best_a)}")

    # Final polish on the global best if time remains.
    if best_a is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline - 90.0:
        final_budget = min(500.0, max(90.0, deadline - time.monotonic() - 60.0))
        try:
            a_fin, sc_fin = _optimize_sequence(best_a, final_budget)
            if np.isfinite(sc_fin) and sc_fin < best_sc:
                best_sc = float(sc_fin)
                best_a = _normalize(a_fin)
                print(f"Final polish improved: {best_sc:.10f}, n={len(best_a)}")
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"final polish failed: {e}")

    if best_a is None or not np.all(np.isfinite(best_a)) or float(np.sum(best_a)) <= 0.01:
        out_seq = [0.5] * 1024
    else:
        out_arr = np.asarray(best_a, dtype=np.float64)
        out_arr = out_arr / float(out_arr.sum())
        out = []
        for v in out_arr:
            if not np.isfinite(v):
                v = 0.0
            if v < 0.0:
                v = 0.0
            elif v > 1000.0:
                v = 1000.0
            out.append(float(v))
        if sum(out) <= 0.01:
            out = [0.5] * 1024

    # Final internal verification; evaluate_sequence should exist in the judge.
    try:
        verified = float(evaluate_sequence(out))
    except Exception:
        verified = best_sc

    elapsed = time.monotonic() - t_start
    print("=" * 70)
    print(f"Final internal best: {best_sc:.12f}")
    print(f"Final verified:      {verified:.12f}")
    print(f"Length: {len(out)}, elapsed: {elapsed:.1f}s / {total_budget:.0f}s")
    print("=" * 70)
    return out
```
